HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_24b35b9d59a5d458aec7","text":"109:4’s person reversal and perfect **عَبَدتُّمْ**, followed by 109:5’s exact return, made 109:3 newly visible as the first side of a bracket rather than a repetitive assertion. The later **دِين** of 109:6 also corrected an act-only reading: `د ي ن B004` lets the participial `ع ب د B001` mechanism describe status under an authority, while the unchanged recurrence confirms that this status relation has not converged.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3a8995d3496e01ab8b26","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:3:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\"]],[\"109:3:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İsim biçimi ile fiilin karşıtlığı, anlık bir eylem uyuşmazlığından öte, muhatapların konuşanın kulluk ettiği varlığa yönelen kalıcı kulluk rolünde bulunmadığını gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7f4a9924dd2830b2b921","text":"3. **The repeated plural resists scattering into exceptions.** ع ب د B010, \"scattering in different directions,\" is activated negatively by the compact plural أَنتُمْ عَٰبِدُونَ and by the later exact repetition of the same ayah in 109:5. Mechanism: the grammar gathers the addressees into one addressed party even though the branch inventory allows scattered groups/routes; the repeated line prevents imagining partial overlap or dispersed exceptions inside the packet. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives a scattering branch and repeated plural structure; I infer that repetition blocks dispersal. Reading change: the ayah reads as a group-level relational denial, not a case-by-case census.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9c3d9d3b0a494f98e118","text":"1. **The noun form shifts the problem from act to role.** The fixed ayah supplies ع ب د B003, \"worship and submissive obedience,\" with عَٰبِدُونَ as a plural active participle and أَعْبُدُ as a first-person verb. The activator is 109:2, where both sides appeared as verbs. Mechanism: after the speaker denies doing their worship, this ayah denies their being the kind of worshippers oriented toward what he worships. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives the form shift from verbs to noun+verb; I infer role-stability from the participle. Reading change: the ayah is not a simple mirror of 109:2; it says the addressees lack a worshipper-identity relative to the speaker's object.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a165988085762e1335cc","text":"109:5 was the decisive surprise for this ayah: its exact repetition of 109:3 made the first occurrence provisional, as though 109:3 opens a role-denial that must be reaffirmed after the speaker's own identity is protected in 109:4. That structure strengthened the reading-change from action to role. 109:6 also made the participle عَٰبِدُونَ feel preparatory for دِينُكُمْ: the addressees' non-participation in the speaker's worship is what allows a separate dīn to be assigned to them. The scattering branch ع ب د B010 remained secondary, but repetition made its negative role clearer.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b084ee1b59640c1ca139","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:3:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B001\"]],[\"109:3:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kulluk rolü bir otorite altında statü de kurduğundan, muhatapların konuşanın bağlı olduğu otorite altında bulunmadıkları yönü belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_be39d8cb92cd52e92546","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:3:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B010\"]],[\"109:3:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Çoğul hitabın dağınık istisnaları dışlayıp muhatapları tek bir ilişki hükmünde topladığı önerisi, uzak bir çağrışıma dayanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c98cc95fee0310565de6","text":"2. **Honor/service is asymmetrical.** ع ب د B006, \"honoring and magnifying,\" is activated by the object phrase مَا أَعْبُدُ and by the basmalah's ء ل ه B001/B002 divine object of worship/name. Mechanism: the issue is not only who performs rituals but who magnifies and serves what is worthy of service. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives worship/honor branches and the opening divine name; I infer that the object of the speaker's worship carries an honor-standard the addressees do not share. Reading change: \"you are not worshippers of what I worship\" becomes \"you do not occupy the honoring-service relation that my worship requires.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cbaa87b5fc767394a9b5","text":"3. **The nominal predicate can describe a status under authority.** Fixed `ع ب د B001` supplies owned servant-status, and contextual `د ي ن B004` in 109:6 supplies subjugation and possession. The explicit pronoun plus participle, **أَنتُمْ عَٰبِدُونَ**, is structurally suited to a role attribution, while the negation prevents that role from attaching to the speaker’s object. The packet supplies status, authority, and grammar; I infer that worship establishes the corresponding governed relation. The line thus also says that the addressees do not stand under the authority served by the speaker.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cbb446fa6e7f8a74017c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:3:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\"]],[\"109:3:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"109:5'te aynen dönmesi, bu ilk cümleyi aradaki ters yönlü sınamanın başlangıç parantezi hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dab28c86f6d8430e6d78","text":"4. **Its first occurrence becomes one side of a textual bracket.** The same fixed clause recurs exactly at 109:5 after 109:4 reverses subject and temporal profile. Direct `ع ب د B003` remains the lexical base; exact repetition assigns invariance to the addressees’ side of the relation. The abductive move is that recurrence after an intervening reversal is deliberate closure. This changes 109:3 from a passing report into the opening boundary of a reciprocal non-convergence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e2d84dcbea10f1fc97fa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:3:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B006\"]],[\"109:3:5\",\"root_000973\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Buradaki kulluk, yalnızca ritüel davranışı değil, kulluk edileni değerli sayıp yüceltmeyi de içerir; ayet bu değer verme ilişkisini de dışarıda bırakır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f85fe84dc6bbd8366393","text":"1. **A settled role is contrasted with an enacted service.** Fixed **عَٰبِدُونَ** and **أَعْبُدُ** both activate `ع ب د B003`, but the first is a plural active participle predicated after **أَنتُمْ**, whereas the second is an imperfect verb. The participle is assigned the role of profiling the addressees as service-agents; the verb profiles the speaker’s service as enacted activity. I infer this role/action distinction from the supplied forms. The denial therefore reaches beyond whether they perform the same act at a moment: it denies that they occupy the servant-role toward what the speaker serves.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f8da41891406bc3345ae","text":"2. **What is absent is recognition expressed as honor.** Fixed `ع ب د B006` supplies honoring, magnifying, and treating something as worthy of service. The basmalah names the speaker’s worship pole, while **مَا أَعْبُدُ** places that pole in the object position of the fixed ayah. This branch assigns **عَٰبِدُونَ** a valuational role: service would enact recognition of the object’s worth. I infer that submissive service carries such valuation. The line can then deny not merely matching ritual behavior but the addressees’ conferral of honor on what the speaker serves.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
